Crates Rust liés dans uv 0.12.24 (d'après Cargo.lock au tag 0.12.24, toutes plateformes confondues).
Les textes Apache 2.0 et MPL 2.0 génériques ne sont pas répétés : voir la fin de THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt
et https://www.mozilla.org/MPL/2.0/.

Composants de uv lui-même (licence de uv) : uv, uv-audit, uv-audit-operations, uv-auth, uv-bench, uv-bin-install, uv-build, uv-build-backend, uv-build-commands, uv-build-frontend, uv-cache, uv-cache-info, uv-cache-key, uv-cli, uv-client, uv-command-support, uv-configuration, uv-console, uv-dev, uv-dirs, uv-dispatch, uv-distribution, uv-distribution-filename, uv-distribution-types, uv-environment-operations, uv-errors, uv-extract, uv-fastid, uv-flags, uv-fs, uv-git, uv-git-types, uv-globfilter, uv-install-operations, uv-install-wheel, uv-installer, uv-keyring, uv-lock, uv-lock-operations, uv-logging, uv-macros, uv-metadata, uv-netrc, uv-normalize, uv-once-map, uv-options-metadata, uv-pep440, uv-pep508, uv-performance-memory-allocator, uv-pip-commands, uv-platform, uv-platform-tags, uv-preview, uv-project-commands, uv-project-edit, uv-publish, uv-publish-commands, uv-pypi-types, uv-python-commands, uv-python-discovery, uv-python-interpreter, uv-python-managed, uv-python-types, uv-redacted, uv-requirements, uv-requirements-txt, uv-resolve-operations, uv-resolver, uv-resolver-types, uv-scripts, uv-settings, uv-shell, uv-small-str, uv-state, uv-static, uv-test, uv-threads, uv-toml, uv-tool, uv-tool-commands, uv-torch, uv-trampoline-builder, uv-types, uv-unix, uv-version, uv-virtualenv, uv-warnings, uv-windows, uv-workspace, uv-workspace-commands
